  device uchar* pending_0 = reinterpret_cast<device uchar*>(channel_0.pending_cell);

  const uint pending_index_1 = lane.channel_slot_offset + dense_1;

  device uchar* pending_1 = reinterpret_cast<device uchar*>(channel_1.pending_cell);
  const uint dense_2 = channel_bindings[dispatch_lane * layout->reserved0 + 2];
  const M3LaneChannelSlot channel_2 = slots[lane.channel_slot_offset + dense_2];
  const uint pending_index_2 = lane.channel_slot_offset + dense_2;
  const device uchar* current_2 = reinterpret_cast<const device uchar*>(pending_flags[pending_index_2] != 0 ? channel_2.pending_cell : channel_2.committed_cell);
  device uchar* pending_2 = reinterpret_cast<device uchar*>(channel_2.pending_cell);

    const uint intrinsic_row_base = 0u;
    if (layout->vocab == 0u || intrinsic_desc.len % layout->vocab != 0u || intrinsic_row_base > row_meta.count || intrinsic_desc.len / layout->vocab > row_meta.count - intrinsic_row_base) { m1_fault(status, 0xA0u); return; }

      const uint column = element % layout->vocab;
      const uint source_row = row_indices[row_meta.offset + intrinsic_row_base + logical_row];

  if (status->state != 1) return;

  if (status->state != 1) return;

    for (uint draft = 0; draft < draft_desc.len; ++draft) {
